{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787350417826,"sessionID":"ses_fd99d15d1ffe7KWwhBCbkwEhww","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"read","callID":"call_01a02662ff5971c0a8d7ec83eea0e298","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD"},"output":"<path>/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD</path>\n<type>file</type>\n<content>\n1: # AI Participants — Configuration, Prompts, and Watcher Queuing\n2: \n3: ## Overview\n4: \n5: AI participants are synthetic voters that can cast pairwise rankings on the same\n6: sort-groups human participants see. They are **system-level** (not end-user\n7: editable), gated by `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS`, and produce a separate **Agents**\n8: baseline that is excluded from human counts in the project report but shown as\n9: `ai_baseline` for comparison. AI work is queued and executed by the background\n10: watcher process rather than the request thread.\n11: \n12: ```\n13: Browser / API  ->  CustomerProject (include_ai_agents, ai_voter_models)\n14:               ->  AiAgentJob  (queue per model_key)\n15:               ->  powerchoice_watcher.py --poll--> process_next_ai_job()\n16:               ->  ProjectVoteParticipant (source=ai, participant_key=ai:..., display_name=\"AI AGENT: ...\")\n17:               ->  ProjectVoteGroupResult / pairings / rank_order\n18:               ->  build_report_with_ai_baseline() splits human vs ai for the UI\n19: ```\n20: \n21: ---\n22: \n23: ## Configuration — system level, not end-user editable\n24: \n25: All knobs live in **`src/config/config_settings.py`** (`GlobalSettings`) and are\n26: read via `pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation.runtime_settings()` (startup YAML\n27: `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS` + `.env`). End users cannot edit prompts; admins configure\n28: providers at the customer level (`/ws/core/customer-ai-settings`) which is\n29: encrypted (`enc:v1:`) and never echoed to the browser context.\n30: \n31: | Setting | Env | Default | Notes |\n32: |---|---|---|---|\n33: | `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS` | startup YAML (`src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`) | `False` | Gate; pvf ClassVar |\n34: | `AI_SERVICE` | `AI_SERVICE` | `opencode_go` | `opencode_go | opencode_zen | openrouter` |\n35: | `AI_API_KEY` | `AI_API_KEY` | `\"\"` | System fallback; customer key preferred |\n36: | `AI_MODEL` | `AI_MODEL` | `glm-5.2` | Default model key |\n37: | `AI_MAX_CONCURRENT_PROJECTS` | `AI_MAX_CONCURRENT_PROJECTS` | `2` | Job claim concurrency |\n38: | `AI_MAX_PAIRS_PER_PROJECT` | `AI_MAX_PAIRS_PER_PROJECT` | `500` | Per-project pair cap |\n39: | `AI_PAIRS_PER_TICK` | `AI_PAIRS_PER_TICK` | `20` | Pairs processed per watcher tick |\n40: | `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` | `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` | `0.25` | Abort group if skip-rate >= threshold |\n41: | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO` | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO` | `0.2` | Combined TIE+SKIP quota = `ceil(group_size * ratio)` |\n42: | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN` | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN` | `1` | Minimum quota, even for small groups |\n43: | `AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | see below | System prompt for overall option comparison |\n44: | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | see below | Prompt when a specific factor/criterion scopes the option comparison |\n45: | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE` | see below | Prompt for factor-importance ranking (criteria group) |\n46: | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP` | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP` | `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.` | Choice line while quota remains |\n47: | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT` | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT` | `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.` | Choice line once quota is reached |\n48: \n49: Resolution order for every setting: explicit arg → `runtime_settings()` →\n50: `src/config/config_settings.py` (`app_settings`) → `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py`\n51: (`pvf_settings`). Helpers in **`src/utils/ai/config.py:16`** (`_runtime_settings`,\n52: `_app_settings`, `_pvf_settings`, `_setting`) implement this.\n53: \n54: Customer-level overrides (encrypted at rest) are resolved by\n55: **`src/utils/ai/config.py:183`** `resolve_llm_credentials()` which prefers\n56: `customer.ai_provider` + `decrypted_ai_api_key()` (`src/pvf/utils/field_encryption.py`)\n57: over the system provider, and normalizes `model_key` (`__default__`).\n58: \n59: Preference locations:\n60: - Framework settings base: `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py:106` / `294`\n61: - App settings + prompt templates: `src/config/config_settings.py:11`\n62: - AI config helpers: `src/utils/ai/config.py:1`\n63: - Identity / display names: `src/utils/ai/identity.py:7`\n64: \n65: ---\n66: \n67: ## Prompt templates — configurable, format-string based\n68: \n69: Templates are Python `str.format` templates stored in `config_settings.py` and read\n70: by **`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:72`** `_get_templates()`. They are **not** exposed\n71: to project owners. Editing requires a redeploy (or env override); `format`\n72: failures are caught and logged via `utils_show`, falling back to built-in\n73: defaults (`_DEFAULT_SINGLE`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING`).\n74: \n75: ### Ambient parameters (explicit)\n76: \n77: *Single option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n78: `{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}`\n79: `{option2_title}` `{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n80: \n81: *Factor-specific option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n82: `{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor_title}` `{factor_description}`\n83: `{factor_question}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}` `{option2_title}`\n84: `{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n85: \n86: *Factor-importance ranking* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE`):\n87: `{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor1_title}` `{factor1_question}`\n88: `{factor1_description}` `{factor2_title}` `{factor2_question}` `{factor2_description}`\n89: `{choice_line}`\n90: \n91: All templates receive `{choice_line}` which is swapped per quota state (see next\n92: section). `comparison_question` is normalized by\n93: `src/utils/comparison_question.py:4`. Placeholders are documented in\n94: `src/config/config_settings.py` comments and in\n95: `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:124` `pairwise_messages` docstring.\n96: \n97: ### Default prompts\n98: \n99: **Single factor / overall option (no factor):**\n100: ```\n101: You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n102: \n103: Project Context: {project_title}\n104: {project_description}\n105: \n106: Option 1: {option1_title}\n107: {option1_description}\n108: \n109: Option 2: {option2_title}\n110: {option2_description}\n111: \n112: {choice_line}\n113: ```\n114: \n115: **Multiple factors — probe of option with specific factor:**\n116: ```\n117: You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\n118: \n119: Project Context: {project_title}\n120: {project_description}\n121: \n122: Current Factor: {factor_title}\n123: {factor_description}\n124: \n125: {factor_question}\n126: \n127: Option 1: {option1_title}\n128: {option1_description}\n129: \n130: Option 2: {option2_title}\n131: {option2_description}\n132: \n133: {choice_line}\n134: ```\n135: \n136: **Factor-importance ranking (criteria group):**\n137: ```\n138: You are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n139: \n140: Project Context: {project_title}\n141: {project_description}\n142: \n143: Factor 1: {factor1_title}\n144: Factor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\n145: Factor 1 description: {factor1_description}\n146: \n147: Factor 2: {factor2_title}\n148: Factor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\n149: Factor 2 description: {factor2_description}\n150: \n151: {choice_line}\n152: ```\n153: \n154: Choice lines:\n155: - While quota remains: `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.`\n156: - Once quota reached: `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.`\n157: \n158: System messages are intentionally minimal (\"Reply with exactly one token … Never\n159: explain.\") so the templated user content carries the domain context. Error\n160: handling in `_safe_format` (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:93`) logs `ai prompt format\n161: failed; using fallback` via `utils_show` and never crashes the watcher tick.\n162: \n163: Parsing (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:280` `parse_pairwise_choice`) accepts\n164: `1` / `2` / `Option 1` / `Option 2` / `LEFT` / `RIGHT` / `TIE` / `SKIP` (and\n165: synonyms `EQUAL/DRAW`, `UNSURE/PASS/ABSTAIN`, `A/B`) and maps `1→left (Option 1)`,\n166: `2→right (Option 2)`. Legacy `LEFT/RIGHT` is kept for backward compatibility.\n167: \n168: ---\n169: \n170: ## Randomization vs. 1/2 labeling\n171: \n172: Options (or factors in a criteria group) are randomized per pairing but always\n173: presented as **Option 1 then Option 2**. The response token `1` means \"the item\n174: shown as Option 1\" and `2` means \"Option 2\". Stability of results is preserved\n175: by mapping the randomized presentation back to stable IDs.\n176: \n177: - Scheduler picks canonical pair `nxt = (a,b)` via `src/utils/pair_scheduler.py:261`\n178:   `select_next_pair`.\n179: - `choose_orientation(a,b, history, seed_parts)` (`pair_scheduler.py:301`)\n180:   randomizes orientation deterministically (left-presentation balance + seeded\n181:   50/50) → `(left, right)` where `left` is shown as **Option 1**.\n182: - `pairwise_messages` labels `left` as Option 1 and `right` as Option 2.\n183: - `parse_pairwise_choice` maps `left(1) / right(2)` → `winner_id` =\n184:   `left` or `right`; the stored `PairAnswer` (`pair_scheduler.py:14`) retains\n185:   both canonical `(item_a,item_b)` and presentation `(presented_left_id,\n186:   presented_right_id)`, so `answer_to_pairing` / `pairings_to_answers` round-trip\n187:   without losing orientation. The worker uses this to build `pairings` with\n188:   `winner_id/loser_id` + `presented_left_id/presented_right_id` for the\n189:   BT/Copeland ranking.\n190: \n191: ---\n192: \n193: ## TIE / SKIP quota (combined, per group)\n194: \n195: - Quota per **group** (one sort group in `ProjectVoteGroupResult`) over the\n196:   answers accumulated so far in that group (`SchedulerState.answers`).\n197: - Size = `len(item_ids_initial)` (number of items in the group).\n198: - Quota = `max(AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN, ceil(group_size * AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO))`.\n199:   Default ratio `0.2` with min `1` → a 6-item group allows 2 TIE/SKIP, a 2-item\n200:   group allows 1.\n201: - Implementation: `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:9` `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` /\n202:   `tie_skip_quota` + `combined_abstentions` + `quota_reached`; the worker\n203:   (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:260`) recomputes `combined = combined_abstentions(state.answers)`\n204:   before each LLM call and sets `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`. When\n205:   `threshold==0` (degenerate), abstention is never allowed.\n206: - Prompt switch: `allow_skip_tie=True` → choice line with `TIE or SKIP`;\n207:   `False` → strict `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.` (both `prompts.py` and\n208:   the system wrapper change). If the model still returns `TIE`/`SKIP` after the\n209:   quota is exhausted, it is recorded as `tie`/`skipped` and increments the\n210:   counter (logged via `utils_show`); the next iteration will remain strict, so\n211:   additional abstentions are rate-limited but not silently coerced to a winner.\n212:   Treat this as \"quota is a prompt gate, not a hard reject\" — SKIP is\n213:   **not** valid once the prompt has switched, and callers should expect rate\n214:   limiting rather than re-queueing.\n215: - Global skip guard: `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` (`src/utils/ai/config.py:122`) aborts\n216:   a job if overall `skip_count/pair_count` ≥ `0.25` and all answers are skips\n217:   (see `worker.py:191`, `worker.py:356`).\n218: \n219: ---\n220: \n221: ## Queuing and watcher flow\n222: \n223: ### Enqueue\n224: \n225: - UI toggles `CustomerProject.include_ai_agents` + `ai_voter_models` (deduped via\n226:   `unique_voter_models`, default key `__default__`) in\n227:   `src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:258` / `src/api` customer-project create/update.\n228: - User action `POST /ws/project-votes/ai-baseline-run` (gated by\n229:   `ai_features_enabled` and `include_ai_agents`) enqueues one\n230:   `AiAgentJob` per distinct `model_key` via `AiAgentJob.enqueue_baseline(...)`\n231:   (`src/db/models/ai_agent_jobs.py`). Dedupe prevents double-queue.\n232: \n233: ### Storage\n234: \n235: - `AiAgentJob` row: `customer_id`, `project_id`, `model_key`, `status`\n236:   (`queued→in_progress→complete/failed`), `participant_id`, `pair_count`,\n237:   `skip_count`, `group_count`, `error_summary`. Claim is via\n238:   `AiAgentJob.claim_next(session, max_concurrent)` (ordered by `modify_date`\n239:   for round-robin fairness; see `src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:602`).\n240: \n241: ### Watcher process\n242: \n243: - Entrypoint: `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:1`. Separate OS process (supervisord\n244:   in prod; **not** `start-server.sh`). It loads startup config\n245:   (`src/pvf/bindings/pvf_startup_config.py`) and registers hooks via\n246:   `src/app_shell.py:register_app_hooks`.\n247: - Loop: `process_watch_loop` (`powerchoice_watcher.py:57`) cycles\n248:   `WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST` with exponential back-off\n249:   (`WATCHER_SHORTER_BUSY_POLL_SLEEP`, `WATCHER_LONGER_IDLE_INCREASING_POLL_SLEEP`,\n250:   `WATCHER_POLL_EXPONENTIAL_FACTOR`, `WATCHER_POLL_MAXIMUM_SLEEP_TIME`).\n251: - On the AI tag (`settings.WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG`), each iteration\n252:   calls `process_next_ai_job(session)` (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:105`). If it\n253:   returns `True`, idle counter resets and the loopBusy-waits; else it backs off.\n254: - `process_next_ai_job` claims one job (`ai_max_concurrent_projects`) and runs\n255:   `_run_job`. Retries on `AiProviderError(retryable=True)` by returning `True`\n256:   without advancing counts (`worker.py:313`). Non-retryable errors become\n257:   `skipped` responses.\n258: \n259: ### Per-job execution (`_run_job`)\n260: \n261: 1. Load `CustomerProject` + alternatives/factors (`CustomerProjectAlternatives`,\n262:    `CustomerProjectFactors`) and resolve LLM credentials (`resolve_llm_credentials`).\n263: 2. Get-or-create AI participant (`ProjectVoteParticipant.get_or_create_ai_participant`,\n264:    `source=ai`, `participant_key=ai:default` etc. via `src/utils/ai/identity.py`).\n265: 3. Pick next sort group via `pick_next_sort_group` (`src/utils/vote_sort_session.py`).\n266: 4. If no group / session complete → mark complete or fail on skip-rate.\n267: 5. Issue `ProjectVoteGroupResult.issue_group_result` and build `SchedulerState`\n268:    from `prior_answers` + `answers`.\n269: 6. **Tick loop** (up to `ai_pairs_per_tick`, `ai_max_pairs_per_project` cap):\n270:    `select_next_pair` → `choose_orientation` (randomize) → `pairwise_messages`\n271:    (quota-aware) → `chat_text_sync` (provider: `src/utils/ai/provider.py`) →\n272:    `parse_pairwise_choice` → `PairAnswer` (keep stable + presented IDs) →\n273:    `save_group_pairings`. Repeat until `select_next_pair` is `None`.\n274: 7. Build final order `rank_order_from_answers` → `create_group_result` →\n275:    mark `group_count` and return `True` to let the watcher re-claim the same job\n276:    for its next group. When no groups remain, job goes `complete`.\n277: \n278: Report partitioning: `src/utils/ai/identity.py:61` `partition_vote_data` and\n279: `src/utils/ai/report.py:build_report_with_ai_baseline` keep AI participants\n280: out of `unique_participants` and emit `ai_baseline` (`available`, `option_ranking`,\n281: `factor_rankings`) for the UI. Providers are verified by\n282: `src/pvf/utils/llm_client.py` (`verify_llm_provider`).\n283: \n284: ---\n285: \n286: ## Files\n287: \n288: - `src/config/config_settings.py:11` — app settings + prompt templates + quota knobs\n289: - `src/utils/ai/config.py:1` — `ai_features_enabled`, credential resolution\n290: - `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:1` — templates, `ai_vote_threshold`, `combined_abstentions`, `pairwise_messages`, `parse_pairwise_choice`\n291: - `src/utils/ai/worker.py:1` — `process_next_ai_job`, `_run_job`, scheduler integration, randomization + quota wiring\n292: - `src/utils/ai/provider.py` — `chat_text_sync` / `chat_json_sync` (opencode_go / openrouter)\n293: - `src/utils/ai/identity.py:7` — AI keys/display names + report partitioning\n294: - `src/utils/pair_scheduler.py:14` — `PairAnswer`, `SchedulerState`, `select_next_pair`, `choose_orientation`, `rank_order_from_answers`\n295: - `src/utils/comparison_question.py:4` — question normalization\n296: - `src/db/models/ai_agent_jobs.py` — job queue model\n297: - `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:57` — `process_watch_loop` + AI tag polling\n298: - `src/pvf/utils/callback_delivery.py` — sibling watcher machinery (API callbacks)\n\n(End of file - total 298 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# AI Participants — Configuration, Prompts, and Watcher Queuing\n\n## Overview\n\nAI participants are synthetic voters that can cast pairwise rankings on the same\nsort-groups human participants see. They are **system-level** (not end-user\neditable), gated by `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS`, and produce a separate **Agents**\nbaseline that is excluded from human counts in the project report but shown as\n`ai_baseline` for comparison. AI work is queued and executed by the background\nwatcher process rather than the request thread.\n\n```\nBrowser / API  ->  CustomerProject (include_ai_agents, ai_voter_models)\n              ->  AiAgentJob  (queue per model_key)\n              ->  powerchoice_watcher.py --poll--> process_next_ai_job()\n              ->  ProjectVoteParticipant (source=ai, participant_key=ai:..., display_name=\"AI AGENT: ...\")\n              ->  ProjectVoteGroupResult / pairings / rank_order\n              ->  build_report_with_ai_baseline() splits human vs ai for the UI\n```\n","truncated":false,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD","text":"# AI Participants — Configuration, Prompts, and Watcher Queuing\n\n## Overview\n\nAI participants are synthetic voters that can cast pairwise rankings on the same\nsort-groups human participants see. They are **system-level** (not end-user\neditable), gated by `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS`, and produce a separate **Agents**\nbaseline that is excluded from human counts in the project report but shown as\n`ai_baseline` for comparison. AI work is queued and executed by the background\nwatcher process rather than the request thread.\n\n```\nBrowser / API  ->  CustomerProject (include_ai_agents, ai_voter_models)\n              ->  AiAgentJob  (queue per model_key)\n              ->  powerchoice_watcher.py --poll--> process_next_ai_job()\n              ->  ProjectVoteParticipant (source=ai, participant_key=ai:..., display_name=\"AI AGENT: ...\")\n              ->  ProjectVoteGroupResult / pairings / rank_order\n              ->  build_report_with_ai_baseline() splits human vs ai for the UI\n```\n\n---\n\n## Configuration — system level, not end-user editable\n\nAll knobs live in **`src/config/config_settings.py`** (`GlobalSettings`) and are\nread via `pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation.runtime_settings()` (startup YAML\n`ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS` + `.env`). End users cannot edit prompts; admins configure\nproviders at the customer level (`/ws/core/customer-ai-settings`) which is\nencrypted (`enc:v1:`) and never echoed to the browser context.\n\n| Setting | Env | Default | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `ACTIVATE_AI_AGENTS` | startup YAML (`src/pvf_app_startup.yaml`) | `False` | Gate; pvf ClassVar |\n| `AI_SERVICE` | `AI_SERVICE` | `opencode_go` | `opencode_go | opencode_zen | openrouter` |\n| `AI_API_KEY` | `AI_API_KEY` | `\"\"` | System fallback; customer key preferred |\n| `AI_MODEL` | `AI_MODEL` | `glm-5.2` | Default model key |\n| `AI_MAX_CONCURRENT_PROJECTS` | `AI_MAX_CONCURRENT_PROJECTS` | `2` | Job claim concurrency |\n| `AI_MAX_PAIRS_PER_PROJECT` | `AI_MAX_PAIRS_PER_PROJECT` | `500` | Per-project pair cap |\n| `AI_PAIRS_PER_TICK` | `AI_PAIRS_PER_TICK` | `20` | Pairs processed per watcher tick |\n| `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` | `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` | `0.25` | Abort group if skip-rate >= threshold |\n| `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO` | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO` | `0.2` | Combined TIE+SKIP quota = `ceil(group_size * ratio)` |\n| `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN` | `AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN` | `1` | Minimum quota, even for small groups |\n| `AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | see below | System prompt for overall option comparison |\n| `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE` | see below | Prompt when a specific factor/criterion scopes the option comparison |\n| `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE` | `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE` | see below | Prompt for factor-importance ranking (criteria group) |\n| `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP` | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP` | `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.` | Choice line while quota remains |\n| `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT` | `AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT` | `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.` | Choice line once quota is reached |\n\nResolution order for every setting: explicit arg → `runtime_settings()` →\n`src/config/config_settings.py` (`app_settings`) → `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py`\n(`pvf_settings`). Helpers in **`src/utils/ai/config.py:16`** (`_runtime_settings`,\n`_app_settings`, `_pvf_settings`, `_setting`) implement this.\n\nCustomer-level overrides (encrypted at rest) are resolved by\n**`src/utils/ai/config.py:183`** `resolve_llm_credentials()` which prefers\n`customer.ai_provider` + `decrypted_ai_api_key()` (`src/pvf/utils/field_encryption.py`)\nover the system provider, and normalizes `model_key` (`__default__`).\n\nPreference locations:\n- Framework settings base: `src/pvf/config/pvf_config_settings.py:106` / `294`\n- App settings + prompt templates: `src/config/config_settings.py:11`\n- AI config helpers: `src/utils/ai/config.py:1`\n- Identity / display names: `src/utils/ai/identity.py:7`\n\n---\n\n## Prompt templates — configurable, format-string based\n\nTemplates are Python `str.format` templates stored in `config_settings.py` and read\nby **`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:72`** `_get_templates()`. They are **not** exposed\nto project owners. Editing requires a redeploy (or env override); `format`\nfailures are caught and logged via `utils_show`, falling back to built-in\ndefaults (`_DEFAULT_SINGLE`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING`).\n\n### Ambient parameters (explicit)\n\n*Single option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}`\n`{option2_title}` `{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n\n*Factor-specific option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor_title}` `{factor_description}`\n`{factor_question}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}` `{option2_title}`\n`{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n\n*Factor-importance ranking* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor1_title}` `{factor1_question}`\n`{factor1_description}` `{factor2_title}` `{factor2_question}` `{factor2_description}`\n`{choice_line}`\n\nAll templates receive `{choice_line}` which is swapped per quota state (see next\nsection). `comparison_question` is normalized by\n`src/utils/comparison_question.py:4`. Placeholders are documented in\n`src/config/config_settings.py` comments and in\n`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:124` `pairwise_messages` docstring.\n\n### Default prompts\n\n**Single factor / overall option (no factor):**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Multiple factors — probe of option with specific factor:**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nCurrent Factor: {factor_title}\n{factor_description}\n\n{factor_question}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Factor-importance ranking (criteria group):**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nFactor 1: {factor1_title}\nFactor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\nFactor 1 description: {factor1_description}\n\nFactor 2: {factor2_title}\nFactor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\nFactor 2 description: {factor2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\nChoice lines:\n- While quota remains: `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.`\n- Once quota reached: `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.`\n\nSystem messages are intentionally minimal (\"Reply with exactly one token … Never\nexplain.\") so the templated user content carries the domain context. Error\nhandling in `_safe_format` (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:93`) logs `ai prompt format\nfailed; using fallback` via `utils_show` and never crashes the watcher tick.\n\nParsing (`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:280` `parse_pairwise_choice`) accepts\n`1` / `2` / `Option 1` / `Option 2` / `LEFT` / `RIGHT` / `TIE` / `SKIP` (and\nsynonyms `EQUAL/DRAW`, `UNSURE/PASS/ABSTAIN`, `A/B`) and maps `1→left (Option 1)`,\n`2→right (Option 2)`. Legacy `LEFT/RIGHT` is kept for backward compatibility.\n\n---\n\n## Randomization vs. 1/2 labeling\n\nOptions (or factors in a criteria group) are randomized per pairing but always\npresented as **Option 1 then Option 2**. The response token `1` means \"the item\nshown as Option 1\" and `2` means \"Option 2\". Stability of results is preserved\nby mapping the randomized presentation back to stable IDs.\n\n- Scheduler picks canonical pair `nxt = (a,b)` via `src/utils/pair_scheduler.py:261`\n  `select_next_pair`.\n- `choose_orientation(a,b, history, seed_parts)` (`pair_scheduler.py:301`)\n  randomizes orientation deterministically (left-presentation balance + seeded\n  50/50) → `(left, right)` where `left` is shown as **Option 1**.\n- `pairwise_messages` labels `left` as Option 1 and `right` as Option 2.\n- `parse_pairwise_choice` maps `left(1) / right(2)` → `winner_id` =\n  `left` or `right`; the stored `PairAnswer` (`pair_scheduler.py:14`) retains\n  both canonical `(item_a,item_b)` and presentation `(presented_left_id,\n  presented_right_id)`, so `answer_to_pairing` / `pairings_to_answers` round-trip\n  without losing orientation. The worker uses this to build `pairings` with\n  `winner_id/loser_id` + `presented_left_id/presented_right_id` for the\n  BT/Copeland ranking.\n\n---\n\n## TIE / SKIP quota (combined, per group)\n\n- Quota per **group** (one sort group in `ProjectVoteGroupResult`) over the\n  answers accumulated so far in that group (`SchedulerState.answers`).\n- Size = `len(item_ids_initial)` (number of items in the group).\n- Quota = `max(AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN, ceil(group_size * AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO))`.\n  Default ratio `0.2` with min `1` → a 6-item group allows 2 TIE/SKIP, a 2-item\n  group allows 1.\n- Implementation: `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:9` `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` /\n  `tie_skip_quota` + `combined_abstentions` + `quota_reached`; the worker\n  (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:260`) recomputes `combined = combined_abstentions(state.answers)`\n  before each LLM call and sets `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`. When\n  `threshold==0` (degenerate), abstention is never allowed.\n- Prompt switch: `allow_skip_tie=True` → choice line with `TIE or SKIP`;\n  `False` → strict `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.` (both `prompts.py` and\n  the system wrapper change). If the model still returns `TIE`/`SKIP` after the\n  quota is exhausted, it is recorded as `tie`/`skipped` and increments the\n  counter (logged via `utils_show`); the next iteration will remain strict, so\n  additional abstentions are rate-limited but not silently coerced to a winner.\n  Treat this as \"quota is a prompt gate, not a hard reject\" — SKIP is\n  **not** valid once the prompt has switched, and callers should expect rate\n  limiting rather than re-queueing.\n- Global skip guard: `AI_SKIP_RATE_ABORT` (`src/utils/ai/config.py:122`) aborts\n  a job if overall `skip_count/pair_count` ≥ `0.25` and all answers are skips\n  (see `worker.py:191`, `worker.py:356`).\n\n---\n\n## Queuing and watcher flow\n\n### Enqueue\n\n- UI toggles `CustomerProject.include_ai_agents` + `ai_voter_models` (deduped via\n  `unique_voter_models`, default key `__default__`) in\n  `src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:258` / `src/api` customer-project create/update.\n- User action `POST /ws/project-votes/ai-baseline-run` (gated by\n  `ai_features_enabled` and `include_ai_agents`) enqueues one\n  `AiAgentJob` per distinct `model_key` via `AiAgentJob.enqueue_baseline(...)`\n  (`src/db/models/ai_agent_jobs.py`). Dedupe prevents double-queue.\n\n### Storage\n\n- `AiAgentJob` row: `customer_id`, `project_id`, `model_key`, `status`\n  (`queued→in_progress→complete/failed`), `participant_id`, `pair_count`,\n  `skip_count`, `group_count`, `error_summary`. Claim is via\n  `AiAgentJob.claim_next(session, max_concurrent)` (ordered by `modify_date`\n  for round-robin fairness; see `src/tests/test_ai_agents.py:602`).\n\n### Watcher process\n\n- Entrypoint: `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:1`. Separate OS process (supervisord\n  in prod; **not** `start-server.sh`). It loads startup config\n  (`src/pvf/bindings/pvf_startup_config.py`) and registers hooks via\n  `src/app_shell.py:register_app_hooks`.\n- Loop: `process_watch_loop` (`powerchoice_watcher.py:57`) cycles\n  `WATCHER_PROCESS_MODULE_LIST` with exponential back-off\n  (`WATCHER_SHORTER_BUSY_POLL_SLEEP`, `WATCHER_LONGER_IDLE_INCREASING_POLL_SLEEP`,\n  `WATCHER_POLL_EXPONENTIAL_FACTOR`, `WATCHER_POLL_MAXIMUM_SLEEP_TIME`).\n- On the AI tag (`settings.WATCHER_PROCESS_AI_AGENTS_TAG`), each iteration\n  calls `process_next_ai_job(session)` (`src/utils/ai/worker.py:105`). If it\n  returns `True`, idle counter resets and the loopBusy-waits; else it backs off.\n- `process_next_ai_job` claims one job (`ai_max_concurrent_projects`) and runs\n  `_run_job`. Retries on `AiProviderError(retryable=True)` by returning `True`\n  without advancing counts (`worker.py:313`). Non-retryable errors become\n  `skipped` responses.\n\n### Per-job execution (`_run_job`)\n\n1. Load `CustomerProject` + alternatives/factors (`CustomerProjectAlternatives`,\n   `CustomerProjectFactors`) and resolve LLM credentials (`resolve_llm_credentials`).\n2. Get-or-create AI participant (`ProjectVoteParticipant.get_or_create_ai_participant`,\n   `source=ai`, `participant_key=ai:default` etc. via `src/utils/ai/identity.py`).\n3. Pick next sort group via `pick_next_sort_group` (`src/utils/vote_sort_session.py`).\n4. If no group / session complete → mark complete or fail on skip-rate.\n5. Issue `ProjectVoteGroupResult.issue_group_result` and build `SchedulerState`\n   from `prior_answers` + `answers`.\n6. **Tick loop** (up to `ai_pairs_per_tick`, `ai_max_pairs_per_project` cap):\n   `select_next_pair` → `choose_orientation` (randomize) → `pairwise_messages`\n   (quota-aware) → `chat_text_sync` (provider: `src/utils/ai/provider.py`) →\n   `parse_pairwise_choice` → `PairAnswer` (keep stable + presented IDs) →\n   `save_group_pairings`. Repeat until `select_next_pair` is `None`.\n7. Build final order `rank_order_from_answers` → `create_group_result` →\n   mark `group_count` and return `True` to let the watcher re-claim the same job\n   for its next group. When no groups remain, job goes `complete`.\n\nReport partitioning: `src/utils/ai/identity.py:61` `partition_vote_data` and\n`src/utils/ai/report.py:build_report_with_ai_baseline` keep AI participants\nout of `unique_participants` and emit `ai_baseline` (`available`, `option_ranking`,\n`factor_rankings`) for the UI. Providers are verified by\n`src/pvf/utils/llm_client.py` (`verify_llm_provider`).\n\n---\n\n## Files\n\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:11` — app settings + prompt templates + quota knobs\n- `src/utils/ai/config.py:1` — `ai_features_enabled`, credential resolution\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:1` — templates, `ai_vote_threshold`, `combined_abstentions`, `pairwise_messages`, `parse_pairwise_choice`\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:1` — `process_next_ai_job`, `_run_job`, scheduler integration, randomization + quota wiring\n- `src/utils/ai/provider.py` — `chat_text_sync` / `chat_json_sync` (opencode_go / openrouter)\n- `src/utils/ai/identity.py:7` — AI keys/display names + report partitioning\n- `src/utils/pair_scheduler.py:14` — `PairAnswer`, `SchedulerState`, `select_next_pair`, `choose_orientation`, `rank_order_from_answers`\n- `src/utils/comparison_question.py:4` — question normalization\n- `src/db/models/ai_agent_jobs.py` — job queue model\n- `src/powerchoice_watcher.py:57` — `process_watch_loop` + AI tag polling\n- `src/pvf/utils/callback_delivery.py` — sibling watcher machinery (API callbacks)","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":298,"totalLines":298,"truncated":false}},"title":"docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD","time":{"start":1787350417654,"end":1787350417803}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02662ff5971c0a8d7ec83eea0e298"}},"id":"prt_0266300a60015wQ8Dp1QBXxyeJ","sessionID":"ses_fd99d15d1ffe7KWwhBCbkwEhww","messageID":"msg_02662f262001mg5JnTHRaoO45D"}}

Threshold 0 only for n<=0\n19:     (return 0 so caller can disallow abstention entirely).\n20:     \"\"\"\n21:     try:\n22:         n = int(group_size or 0)\n23:     except (TypeError, ValueError):\n24:         n = 0\n25:     if n <= 0:\n26:         return 0\n27:     ratio = 0.2\n28:     min_q = 1\n29:     try:\n30:         from ...config.config_settings import settings as _s\n31:         ratio = float(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\", 0.2) or 0.2)\n32:         min_q = int(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\", 1) or 1)\n33:     except Exception:\n34:         pass\n35:     # alias for clarity — spec wording\n52: tie_skip_quota = ai_vote_threshold\n53: \n54: \n55: def combined_abstentions(answers: list[Any] | None) -> int:\n56:     \"\"\"Count combined skip+tie (inclusive of 'unsure') in a group.\"\"\"\n57:     if not answers:\n58:         return 0\n59:     cnt = 0\n60:     for a in answers:\n61:         resp = getattr(a, \"response\", None) if not isinstance(a, dict) else a.get(\"response\")\n62:         if resp is None:\n63:             continue\n64:         r = str(resp).lower()\n65:         if r in (\"skipped\", \"skip\", \"tie\", \"unsure\"):\n66:             cnt += 1\n67:     return cnt\n68: \n69: \n70: def quota_reached(group_size: int, abstentions: int) -> bool:\n71:     thresh = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)\n72:     if thresh <= 0:\n73:         return True  # parse_pairwise_choice(text: str) -> str | None:\n341:     \"\"\"Parse LLM token to left/right/tie/skipped.\n342: \n343:     Accepts: 1 / 2 / Option 1 / Option 2 / LEFT / RIGHT / TIE / SKIP (and synonyms).\n344:     Returns \"left\" for Option 1, \"right\" for Option 2, \"tie\", \"skipped\", or None (unknown).\n345:     \"\"\"\n346:     raw = (text or \"\").strip()\n347:     if not raw:\n348:         return None\n349:     # first token plus full-text checks\n350:     first = raw.split()[0].strip().upper().strip(\".,:;\\\"'`\")\n351:     # numeric / option forms\n352:     if first in (\"1\", \"OPTION1\", \"OPTION_1\"):\n353:         return \"left\"\n354:     if first in (\"2\", \"OPTION2\", \"OPTION_2\"):\n355:         return \"right\"\n356:     # handle \"Option 1\" as two tokens — check lower\n357:     lowered = raw.strip().lower()\n358:     if lowered.startswith(\"option 1\") or lowered.startswith(\"option1\") or lowered == \"1\":\n359:         return \"left\"\n360:     if lowered.startswith(\"option 2\") or lowered.startswith(\"option2\") or lowered == \"2\":\n361:         return \"right\"\n362:     if first in (\"LEFT\", \"L\", \"WINNER_LEFT\", \"A\"):\n363:         return \"left\"\n364:     if first in (\"RIGHT\", \"R\", \"WINNER_RIGHT\", \"B\"):\n365:         return \"right\"\n366:     if first in (\"TIE\", \"EQUAL\", \"DRAW\"):\n367:         return \"tie\"\n368:     if first in (\"SKIP\", \"SKIPPED\", \"UNSURE\", \"PASS\", \"ABSTAIN\"):\n369:         return \"skipped\"\n370:     if lowered.startswith(\"left\"):\n371:         return \"left\"\n372:     if lowered.startswith(\"right\"):\n373:         return \"right\"\n374:     if lowered.startswith(\"tie\"):\n375:         return \"tie\"\n376:     if lowered.startswith(\"skip\") or lowered.startswith(\"unsure\") or lowered.startswith(\"abstain\") or lowered.startswith(\"pass\"):\n377:         return \"skipped\"\n378:     # bare numeric after stripping punctuation\n379:     if Threshold 0 only for n<=0\n    (return 0 so caller can disallow abstention entirely).\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        n = int(group_size or 0)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        n = 0\n    if n <= 0:\n        return 0\n    ratio = 0.2\n    min_q = 1\n    try:\n        from ...config.config_settings import settings as _s\n        ratio = float(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\", 0.2) or 0.2)\n        min_q = int(getattr(_s, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\", 1) or 1)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # also try pvf/runtime overrides if present (mirrors config.py pattern)\n    try:\n        from ...pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import runtime_settings\n        rs = runtime_settings()\n        if rs is not None:\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\"):\n                ratio = float(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_RATIO\") or ratio)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\"):\n                min_q = int(getattr(rs, \"AI_TIE_SKIP_QUOTA_MIN\") or min_q)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    ratio = max(0.01, min(0.9, float(ratio)))\n    min_q = max(1, int(min_q))\n    return max(min_q, math.ceil(n * ratio))\n\n\n# alias for clarity — spec wording\ntie_skip_quota = ai_vote_threshold\n\n\ndef combined_abstentions(answers: list[Any] | None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Count combined skip+tie (inclusive of 'unsure') in a group.\"\"\"\n    if not answers:\n        return 0\n    cnt = 0\n    for a in answers:\n        resp = getattr(a, \"response\", None) if not isinstance(a, dict) else a.get(\"response\")\n        if resp is None:\n            continue\n        r = str(resp).lower()\n        if r in (\"skipped\", \"skip\", \"tie\", \"unsure\"):\n            cnt += 1\n    return cnt\n\n\ndef quota_reached(group_size: int, abstentions: int) -> bool:\n    thresh = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)\n    if thresh <= 0:\n        return True  # no abstentions allowed for degenerate groups\n    return int(abstentions or 0) >= thresh\n\n\n# --- template resolution + safe formatting -----------------------------------\n\n_DEFAULT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP = \"Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.\"\n_DEFAULT_CHOICE_STRICT = \"Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.\"\n\n_DEFAULT_SINGLE = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n    \"Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. \"\n    \"This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor \"\n    \"and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Current Factor: {factor_title}\\n{factor_description}\\n\\n{factor_question}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 1: {option1_title}\\n{option1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Option 2: {option2_title}\\n{option2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING = (\n    \"You are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a \"\n    \"weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information \"\n    \"provided and your prior knowledge.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Project Context: {project_title}\\n{project_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Factor 1: {factor1_title}\\nFactor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\\nFactor 1 description: {factor1_description}\\n\\n\"\n    \"Factor 2: {factor2_title}\\nFactor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\\nFactor 2 description: {factor2_description}\\n\\n{choice_line}\"\n)\n\n\ndef _get_templates() -> tuple[str, str, str, str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Return (single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, choice_tie_skip, choice_strict) from settings.\"\"\"\n    single = _DEFAULT_SINGLE\n    factor_opt = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n    factor_rank = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING\n    tie_skip = _DEFAULT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\n    strict = _DEFAULT_CHOICE_STRICT\n    try:\n        from ...config.config_settings import settings as app_s\n        single = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", single) or single)\n        factor_opt = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE\", factor_opt) or factor_opt)\n        factor_rank = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE\", factor_rank) or factor_rank)\n        tie_skip = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\", tie_skip) or tie_skip)\n        strict = v = getattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_TIE_SKIP\")\n                if v:\n                    tie_skip = str(v)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_PROMPT_CHOICE_STRICT\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, _safe_format(template: str, params: dict[str, Any], fallback: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Format template with params; on failure log and return fallback formatted with same params.\"\"\"\n    try:\n failed; using fallback\",\n                error=str(ex) or type(ex).__name__,\n                template_preview=str(template)[:300],\n                missing_keys=str(sorted(params.keys()))[:400],\n = (title or \"\").strip()\n    problem_description = (description or \"\").strip()\n    system = (\n        \"You suggest comparison factors for a decision problem. \"\n        \"Return JSON only, no markdown. \"\n        \"Schema: {\\\"factors\\\": [{\\\"title\\\": string, \\\"description\\\": string}]}. \"\n        \"Produce 5 to 8 distinct factors. Do not include polarity, scores, or rankings.\"\n    )\n    user = (\n        f\"Problem title: {problem_title}\\n\"\n        f\"Problem description: {problem_description}\\n\"\n        \"Suggest the factors that would most help people compare options.\"\n    )\n    return [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user},\n    ]\n\n\ndef pairwise_messages(\n    *,\n    problem_title: str,\n    problem_description: str,\n    left_title: str,\n    left_description: str | None,\n    right_title: str,\n    right_description: str | None,\n    factor_title: str | None,\n    factor_description: str | None,\n    group_type: str,\n    comparison_question: str | None = None,\n    allow_skip_tie: bool = True,\n    # optional explicit ambient for factor-ranking (when caller has them)\n    left_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    left_factor_description: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_description: str | None = None,\n) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Build LLM chat messages for one pairwise vote.\n\n    Randomization is handled by the caller (worker.choose_orientation) which\n    decides which stable item maps to Option 1 (left) vs Option 2 (right).\n    This function always labels the randomized presentation as Option 1 then\n    Option 2, and the caller maps \"1\"->left / \"2\"->right back to stable IDs.\n\n    Ambient placeholders are explicit (see config_settings.py):\n      single: project_title, project_description, option1_title, option1_description,\n              option2_title, option2_description, choice_line\n      factor-option: + factor_title, factor_description, factor_question\n      factor-ranking: project_title, project_description, factor1_title, factor1_question,\n                      factor1_description, factor2_title, factor2_question, factor2_description\n    \"\"\"\n    question = normalize_comparison_question(comparison_question)\n    single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, tie_skip_line, strict_line = _get_templates()\n    choice_line = tie_skip_line if allow_skip_tie else strict_line\n\n    is_criteria = str(group_type).strip().lower() in (\"criteria\", \"factor\", \"factors\")\n\n    # ---- criteria / factor-importance ranking ---------------------------------\n    if is_criteria:\n        # left/right are factors being ranked. Use factor1_* / factor2_* .\n        # Caller passes left_title/right_title as factor titles; factor_description/question may be in extra args\n        # or left_description/right_description.\n        f1_title = str(left_title or \"\").strip()\n        f2_title = str(right_title or \"\").strip()\n        f1_desc = str(left_factor_description if left_factor_description is not None else (left_description or \"\")).strip()\n        f2_desc = str(right_factor_description if right_factor_description is not None else (right_description or \"\")).strip()\n        f1_q = normalize_comparison_question(left_factor_question if left_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n        f2_q = normalize_comparison_question(right_factor_question if right_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n        # Fallback: if no per-factor questions supplied, try factor_title's question (not applicable here)\n        params = {\n            \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor1_title\": f1_title,\n            \"factor1_question\": f1_q,\n            \"factor1_description\": f1_desc,\n            \"factor2_title\": f2_title,\n            \"factor2_question\": f2_q,\n            \"factor2_description\": f2_desc,\n            \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n        }\n        content = _safe_format(factor_rank_tpl, params, _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING.format(**params) if _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING else factor_rank_tpl)\n        # keep a tiny system wrapper for provider compatibility; primary instruction lives in user content per spec\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n        if not allow_skip_tie:\n            system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n        return [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n        ]\n\n    # ---- factor-specific vs single option comparison --------------------------\n    if factor_title:\n        params = {\n            \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_title\": str(factor_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_description\": str(factor_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_question\": str(question or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n        }\n        fallback = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n        tpl = factor_opt_tpl\n        # fallback formatting uses default template directly to guarantee success\n        try:\n            content_try = tpl.format(**params)\n        except Exception as ex:\n            try:\n                from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n                ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt factor-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            content_try = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION.format(**params)\n        content = content_try\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n        return [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n        ]\n\n    # ---- single factor / overall option comparison ---------------------------\n    params = {\n        \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n    }\n    try:\n        content = single_tpl.format(**params)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        try:\n            from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n            ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt single-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        content = _DEFAULT_SINGLE.format(**params)\n    system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n    if not allow_skip_tie:\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Never explain.\"\n    return [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n    ]\n\n\ndef parse_pairwise_choice(text: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Parse LLM token to left/right/tie/skipped.\n\n    Accepts: 1 / 2 / Option 1 / Option 2 / LEFT / RIGHT / TIE / SKIP (and synonyms).\n    Returns \"left\" for Option 1, \"right\" for Option 2, \"tie\", \"skipped\", or None (unknown).\n    \"\"\"\n    raw = (text or \"\").strip()\n    if not raw:\n        return None\n    # first token plus full-text checks\n    first = raw.split()[0].strip().upper().strip(\".,:;\\\"'`\")\n    # numeric / option forms\n    if first in (\"1\", \"OPTION1\", \"OPTION_1\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if first in (\"2\", \"OPTION2\", \"OPTION_2\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    # handle \"Option 1\" as two tokens — check lower\n    lowered = raw.strip().lower()\n    if lowered.startswith(\"option 1\") or lowered.startswith(\"option1\") or lowered == \"1\":\n        return \"left\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"option 2\") or lowered.startswith(\"option2\") or lowered == \"2\":\n        return \"right\"\n    if first in (\"LEFT\", \"L\", \"WINNER_LEFT\", \"A\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if first in (\"RIGHT\", \"R\", \"WINNER_RIGHT\", \"B\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    if first in (\"TIE\", \"EQUAL\", \"DRAW\"):\n        return \"tie\"\n    if first in (\"SKIP\", \"SKIPPED\", \"UNSURE\", \"PASS\", \"ABSTAIN\"):\n        return \"skipped\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"left\"):\n        return \"left\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"right\"):\n        return \"right\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"tie\"):\n        return \"tie\"\n    if lowered.startswith(\"skip\") or lowered.startswith(\"unsure\") or lowered.startswith(\"abstain\") or lowered.startswith(\"pass\"):\n        return \"skipped\"\n    # bare numeric after stripping punctuation\n    if first == \"1\":\n        return \"left\"\n    if first == \"2\":\n        return \"right\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef parse_factor_suggestions(payload: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    rows: list = []\n    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n        raw = payload.get(\"factors\") or payload.get(\"suggestions\") or []\n        rows = raw if isinstance(raw, list) else []\n    elif isinstance(payload, list):\n        rows = payload\n    out: list[dict[str, str]] = []\n    seen: set[str] = set()\n    for item in rows:\n        if not isinstance(item, dict):\n            continue\n        title = str(item.get(\"title\") or item.get(\"factor_title\") or \"\").strip()\n        description = str(item.get(\"description\") or item.get(\"factor_description\") or \"\").strip()\n        if not title:\n            continue\n        key = title.lower()\n        if key in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(key)\n        out.append({\"title\": title[:200], \"description\": description[:2000]})\n        if len(out) >= 8:\n            break\n    return out\n\n\n# --- compare_prompt rewrite helpers ------------------------------------------\n\n_DEFAULT_OPTION_REWRITE = (\n    \"Rewrite title+description as 2-5 word consistent label for pairwise comparison; \"\n    \"no trailing punctuation; preserve meaning; return JSON {\\\"compare_prompt\\\": string}\"\n)\n_DEFAULT_FACTOR_REWRITE = (\n    \"Rewrite as concise question 'Which ...?' <=120 chars; respect polarity (less-is-better vs more-is-better) \"\n    \"and temporal cue; return JSON {\\\"compare_prompt\\\": string}\"\n)\n\n\ndef _rewrite_templates() -> tuple[str, str]:\n    opt = _DEFAULT_OPTION_REWRITE\n    fac = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_REWRITE\n    try:\n        from ...config.config_settings import settings as app_s\n        opt = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_REWRITE_OPTION_PROMPT\", opt) or opt)\n        fac = str(getattr(app_s, \"AI_REWRITE_FACTOR_PROMPT\", fac) or fac)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    try:\n        from ...pvf.bindings.pvf_invocation import runtime_settings\n        rs = runtime_settings()\n        if rs is not None:\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_REWRITE_OPTION_PROMPT\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, \"AI_REWRITE_OPTION_PROMPT\")\n                if v:\n                    opt = str(v)\n            if hasattr(rs, \"AI_REWRITE_FACTOR_PROMPT\"):\n                v = getattr(rs, \"AI_REWRITE_FACTOR_PROMPT\")\n                if v:\n                    fac = str(v)\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    return opt, fac\n\n\ndef option_rewrite_messages(title: str, description: str | None) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    opt_tpl, _ = _rewrite_templates()\n    user = (\n        f\"{opt_tpl}\\n\\nTitle: {(title or '').strip()}\\nDescription: {(description or '').strip()}\\n\"\n        \"Return JSON only.\"\n    )\n    system = \"You rewrite option labels for pairwise comparison. Return JSON only, no markdown.\"\n    return [{\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system}, {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user}]\n\n\ndef factor_rewrite_messages(\n    title: str, description: str | None, polarity_positive: bool | None, polarity_note: str | None\n) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    _, fac_tpl = _rewrite_templates()\n    pol = \"more is better\" if polarity_positive else \"less is better\"\n    note = str(polarity_note or \"\").strip()\n    user = (\n        f\"{fac_tpl}\\n\\nTitle: {(title or '').strip()}\\nDescription: {(description or '').strip()}\\n\"\n        f\"Polarity: {pol}\\n\"\n        f\"Polarity note: {note}\\n\"\n        \"Return JSON only.\"\n    )\n    system = \"You rewrite factor wording as concise 'Which ...?' question. Return JSON only, no markdown.\"\n    return [{\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system}, {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user}]\n\n\ndef parse_compare_prompt(payload: Any) -> str | None:\n    raw = None\n    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n        raw = payload.get(\"compare_prompt\") or payload.get(\"prompt\") or payload.get(\"text\") or payload.get(\"value\")\n    elif isinstance(payload, str):\n        raw = payload\n    if raw is None:\n        return None\n    txt = str(raw).strip()\n    if not txt:\n        return None\n    return normalize_compare_prompt(txt)","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":487,"totalLines":487,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/utils/ai/prompts.py","time":{"start":1787350417638,"end":1787350417897}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02662feef7892806e70426134a1b7"}},"id":"prt_02663009a001C37uaY0qKu6HgY","sessionID":"ses_fd99d15d1ffe7KWwhBCbkwEhww","messageID":"msg_02662f262001mg5JnTHRaoO45D"}}

optional explicit ambient for factor-ranking (when caller has them)\n219:     left_factor_question: str | None = None,\n220:     right_factor_question: str | None = None,\n221:     left_factor_description: str | None = None,\n222:     right_factor_description: str | None = None,\n223: ) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n224:     \"\"\"Build LLM chat messages for one pairwise vote.\n225: \n226:     Randomization is handled by the caller (worker.choose_orientation) which\n227:     decides which stable item maps to Option 1 (left) vs Option 2 (right).\n228:     This function always labels the randomized presentation as Option 1 then\n229:     Option 2, and the caller maps \"1\"->left / \"2\"->right back to stable IDs.\n230: \n231:     Ambient placeholders are explicit (see config_settings.py):\n232:       single: project_title, project_description, option1_title, option1_description,\n233:               option2_title, option2_description, choice_line\n234:       factor-option: + factor_title, factor_description, factor_question\n235:       factor-ranking: project_title, project_description, factor1_title, factor1_question,\n236:                       factor1_description, factor2_title, factor2_question, factor2_description\n237:     \"\"\"\n238:     question = normalize_comparison_question(comparison_question)\n239:     single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, tie_skip_line, strict_line = _get_templates()\n240:     choice_line = tie_skip_line if allow_skip_tie else strict_line\n241: \n242:     is_criteria = str(group_type).strip().lower() in (\"criteria\", \"factor\", \"factors\")\n243: \n244:     # ---- criteria / factor-importance ranking ---------------------------------\n245:     if is_criteria:\n246:         # left/right are factors being ranked. Use factor1_* / factor2_* .\n247:         # Caller passes left_title/right_title as factor titles; factor_description/question may be in extra args\n248:         # or left_description/right_description.\n249:         f1_title = str(left_title or \"\").strip()\n250:         f2_title = str(right_title or \"\").strip()\n251:         f1_desc = str(left_factor_description if left_factor_description is not None else (left_description or \"\")).strip()\n252:         f2_desc = str(right_factor_description if right_factor_description is not None else (right_description or \"\")).strip()\n253:         f1_q = normalize_comparison_question(left_factor_question if left_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n254:         f2_q = normalize_comparison_question(right_factor_question if right_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n255:         # Fallback: if no per-factor questions supplied, try factor_title's question (not applicable here)\n256:         params = {\n257:             \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n258:             \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n259:             \"factor1_title\": f1_title,\n260:             \"factor1_question\": f1_q,\n261:             \"factor1_description\": f1_desc,\n262:             \"factor2_title\": f2_title,\n263:             \"factor2_question\": f2_q,\n264:             \"factor2_description\": f2_desc,\n265:             \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n266:         }\n267:         content = _safe_format(factor_rank_tpl, params, _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING.format(**params) if _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING else factor_rank_tpl)\n268:         # keep a tiny system wrapper for provider compatibility; primary instruction lives in user content per spec\n269:         system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n270:         if not allow_skip_tie:\n271:             system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n272:         return [\n273:             {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n274:             {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n275:         ]\n276: \n277:     # ---- factor-specific vs single option comparison --------------------------\n278:     if factor_title:\n279:         params = {\n280:             \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n281:             \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n282:             \"factor_title\": str(factor_title or \"\").strip(),\n283:             \"factor_description\": str(factor_description or \"\").strip(),\n284:             \"factor_question\": str(question or \"\").strip(),\n285:             \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n286:             \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n287:             \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n288:             \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n289:             \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n290:         }\n291:         fallback = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n292:         tpl = factor_opt_tpl\n293:         # fallback formatting uses default template directly to guarantee success\n294:         try:\n295:             content_try = tpl.format(**params)\n296:         except Exception as ex:\n297:             try:\n298:                 from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n299:                 ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt factor-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n300:             except Exception:\n301:                 pass\n302:             content_try = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION.format(**params)\n303:         content = content_try\n304:         system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n332:     if not allow_skip_tie:\n333:         system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Use offset=345 to continue.)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"def pairwise_messages(\n    *,\n    problem_title: str,\n    problem_description: str,\n    left_title: str,\n    left_description: str | None,\n    right_title: str,\n    right_description: str | None,\n    factor_title: str | None,\n    factor_description: str | None,\n    group_type: str,\n    comparison_question: str | None = None,\n    allow_skip_tie: bool = True,\n    # optional explicit ambient for factor-ranking (when caller has them)\n    left_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    left_factor_description: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_description: str | None = None,\n) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Build LLM chat messages for one pairwise vote.","truncated":true,"loaded":[],"display":{"type":"file","path":"/workspaces/powerchoice/src/utils/ai/prompts.py","text":"def pairwise_messages(\n    *,\n    problem_title: str,\n    problem_description: str,\n    left_title: str,\n    left_description: str | None,\n    right_title: str,\n    right_description: str | None,\n    factor_title: str | None,\n    factor_description: str | None,\n    group_type: str,\n    comparison_question: str | None = None,\n    allow_skip_tie: bool = True,\n    # optional explicit ambient for factor-ranking (when caller has them)\n    left_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_question: str | None = None,\n    left_factor_description: str | None = None,\n    right_factor_description: str | None = None,\n) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Build LLM chat messages for one pairwise vote.\n\n    Randomization is handled by the caller (worker.choose_orientation) which\n    decides which stable item maps to Option 1 (left) vs Option 2 (right).\n    This function always labels the randomized presentation as Option 1 then\n    Option 2, and the caller maps \"1\"->left / \"2\"->right back to stable IDs.\n\n    Ambient placeholders are explicit (see config_settings.py):\n      single: project_title, project_description, option1_title, option1_description,\n              option2_title, option2_description, choice_line\n      factor-option: + factor_title, factor_description, factor_question\n      factor-ranking: project_title, project_description, factor1_title, factor1_question,\n                      factor1_description, factor2_title, factor2_question, factor2_description\n    \"\"\"\n    question = normalize_comparison_question(comparison_question)\n    single_tpl, factor_opt_tpl, factor_rank_tpl, tie_skip_line, strict_line = _get_templates()\n    choice_line = tie_skip_line if allow_skip_tie else strict_line\n\n    is_criteria = str(group_type).strip().lower() in (\"criteria\", \"factor\", \"factors\")\n\n    # ---- criteria / factor-importance ranking ---------------------------------\n    if is_criteria:\n        # left/right are factors being ranked. Use factor1_* / factor2_* .\n        # Caller passes left_title/right_title as factor titles; factor_description/question may be in extra args\n        # or left_description/right_description.\n        f1_title = str(left_title or \"\").strip()\n        f2_title = str(right_title or \"\").strip()\n        f1_desc = str(left_factor_description if left_factor_description is not None else (left_description or \"\")).strip()\n        f2_desc = str(right_factor_description if right_factor_description is not None else (right_description or \"\")).strip()\n        f1_q = normalize_comparison_question(left_factor_question if left_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n        f2_q = normalize_comparison_question(right_factor_question if right_factor_question is not None else None) or \"\"\n        # Fallback: if no per-factor questions supplied, try factor_title's question (not applicable here)\n        params = {\n            \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor1_title\": f1_title,\n            \"factor1_question\": f1_q,\n            \"factor1_description\": f1_desc,\n            \"factor2_title\": f2_title,\n            \"factor2_question\": f2_q,\n            \"factor2_description\": f2_desc,\n            \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n        }\n        content = _safe_format(factor_rank_tpl, params, _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING.format(**params) if _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING else factor_rank_tpl)\n        # keep a tiny system wrapper for provider compatibility; primary instruction lives in user content per spec\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n        if not allow_skip_tie:\n            system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n        return [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n        ]\n\n    # ---- factor-specific vs single option comparison --------------------------\n    if factor_title:\n        params = {\n            \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_title\": str(factor_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_description\": str(factor_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"factor_question\": str(question or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n            \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n            \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n        }\n        fallback = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION\n        tpl = factor_opt_tpl\n        # fallback formatting uses default template directly to guarantee success\n        try:\n            content_try = tpl.format(**params)\n        except Exception as ex:\n            try:\n                from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n                ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt factor-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            content_try = _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION.format(**params)\n        content = content_try\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n        if not allow_skip_tie:\n            system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n        return [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n        ]\n\n    # ---- single factor / overall option comparison ---------------------------\n    params = {\n        \"project_title\": str(problem_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"project_description\": str(problem_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_title\": str(left_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option1_description\": str(left_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_title\": str(right_title or \"\").strip(),\n        \"option2_description\": str(right_description or \"\").strip(),\n        \"choice_line\": choice_line,\n    }\n    try:\n        content = single_tpl.format(**params)\n    except Exception as ex:\n        try:\n            from ...pvf.utils import utils_show as ut\n            ut.show_vars_semi(\"ai prompt single-option format failed\", error=str(ex)[:300])\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        content = _DEFAULT_SINGLE.format(**params)\n    system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token as instructed. Never explain.\"\n    if not allow_skip_tie:\n        system = \"You are one participant in a pairwise comparison. Reply with exactly one token: 1 or 2 (or Option 1 / Option 2). Never reply TIE or SKIP. Never explain.\"\n    return [\n        {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": system},\n        {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content},\n    ]\n\n\ndef parse_pairwise_choice(text: str) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Parse LLM token to left/right/tie/skipped.\n\n    Accepts: 1 / 2 / Option 1 / Option 2 / LEFT / RIGHT / TIE / SKIP (and synonyms).\n    Returns \"left\" for Option 1, \"right\" for Option 2, \"tie\", \"skipped\", or None (unknown).","lineStart":205,"lineEnd":344,"totalLines":487,"truncated":true}},"title":"src/utils/ai/prompts.py","time":{"start":1787350476139,"end":1787350476390}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a02663e43d7f83b056964ca95cd397"}},"id":"prt_02663e4830011eaBHHU3DA2FxD","sessionID":"ses_fd99d15d1ffe7KWwhBCbkwEhww","messageID":"msg_02663d2c4001a7SAv3Gqvj2upl"}}

Editing requires a redeploy (or env override); `format`\n72: failures are caught and logged via `utils_show`, falling back to built-in\n73: defaults (`_DEFAULT_SINGLE`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING`).\n74: \n75: ### Ambient parameters (explicit)\n76: \n77: *Single option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n78: `{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}`\n79: `{option2_title}` `{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n80: \n81: *Factor-specific option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n82: `{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor_title}` `{factor_description}`\n83: `{factor_question}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}` `{option2_title}`\n84: `{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n85: \n86: *Factor-importance ranking* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE`):\n87: `{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor1_title}` `{factor1_question}`\n88: `{factor1_description}` `{factor2_title}` `{factor2_question}` `{factor2_description}`\n89: `{choice_line}`\n90: \n91: All templates receive `{choice_line}` which is swapped per quota state (see next\n92: section). `comparison_question` is normalized by\n93: `src/utils/comparison_question.py:4`. Placeholders are documented in\n94: `src/config/config_settings.py` comments and in\n95: `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:124` `pairwise_messages` docstring.\n96: \n97: ### Default prompts\n98: \n99: **Single factor / overall option (no factor):**\n100: ```\n101: You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n102: \n103: Project Context: {project_title}\n104: {project_description}\n105: \n106: Option 1: {option1_title}\n107: {option1_description}\n108: \n109: Option 2: {option2_title}\n110: {option2_description}\n111: \n112: {choice_line}\n113: ```\n114: \n115: **Multiple factors — probe of option with specific factor:**\n116: ```\n117: You are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\n118: \n119: Project Context: {project_title}\n120: {project_description}\n121: \n122: Current Factor: {factor_title}\n123: {factor_description}\n124: \n125: {factor_question}\n126: \n127: Option 1: {option1_title}\n128: {option1_description}\n129: \n130: Option 2: {option2_title}\n131: {option2_description}\n132: \n133: {choice_line}\n134: ```\n135: \n136: **Factor-importance ranking (criteria group):**\n137: ```\n138: You are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n139: \n140: Project Context: {project_title}\n141: {project_description}\n142: \n143: Factor 1: {factor1_title}\n144: Factor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\n145: Factor 1 description: {factor1_description}\n146: \n147: Factor 2: {factor2_title}\n148: Factor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\n149: Factor 2 description: {factor2_description}\n150: \n151: {choice_line}\n152: ```\n153: \n154: Choice lines:\n155: - While quota remains: `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. They are **not** exposed\nto project owners. Editing requires a redeploy (or env override); `format`\nfailures are caught and logged via `utils_show`, falling back to built-in\ndefaults (`_DEFAULT_SINGLE`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING`).\n\n### Ambient parameters (explicit)\n\n*Single option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}`\n`{option2_title}` `{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n\n*Factor-specific option comparison* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor_title}` `{factor_description}`\n`{factor_question}` `{option1_title}` `{option1_description}` `{option2_title}`\n`{option2_description}` `{choice_line}`\n\n*Factor-importance ranking* (`AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE`):\n`{project_title}` `{project_description}` `{factor1_title}` `{factor1_question}`\n`{factor1_description}` `{factor2_title}` `{factor2_question}` `{factor2_description}`\n`{choice_line}`\n\nAll templates receive `{choice_line}` which is swapped per quota state (see next\nsection). `comparison_question` is normalized by\n`src/utils/comparison_question.py:4`. Placeholders are documented in\n`src/config/config_settings.py` comments and in\n`src/utils/ai/prompts.py:124` `pairwise_messages` docstring.\n\n### Default prompts\n\n**Single factor / overall option (no factor):**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Multiple factors — probe of option with specific factor:**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nCurrent Factor: {factor_title}\n{factor_description}\n\n{factor_question}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Factor-importance ranking (criteria group):**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a weighted selection ranking process. Choose the more important factor based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nFactor 1: {factor1_title}\nFactor 1 Question Format: {factor1_question}\nFactor 1 description: {factor1_description}\n\nFactor 2: {factor2_title}\nFactor 2 Question Format: {factor2_question}\nFactor 2 description: {factor2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\nChoice lines:\n- While quota remains: `Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP. Use TIE and SKIP sparingly.`\n- Once quota reached: `Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.`\n\nSystem messages are intentionally minimal (\"Reply with exactly one token … Never\nexplain.\") so the templated user content carries the domain context.
