Choose the best overall answer based on information provided and your prior knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Multiple factors — probe of option with specific factor:**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking opinion between the following two options. This ranking is for a specific factor or perspective. Make a selection based on the factor and specific question, keeping the factor in context, based on your overall knowledge.\n\nProject Context: {project_title}\n{project_description}\n\nCurrent Factor: {factor_title}\n{factor_description}\n\n{factor_question}\n\nOption 1: {option1_title}\n{option1_description}\n\nOption 2: {option2_title}\n{option2_description}\n\n{choice_line}\n```\n\n**Factor-importance ranking (criteria group):**\n```\nYou are an agent being asked for a ranking the importance of two factors being used in a weighted selection ranking process. {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1787350530891,"sessionID":"ses_fd99d15d1ffe7KWwhBCbkwEhww","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"bash","callID":"call_01a026648a3e72f2ad32eca4e216efe6","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"command":"PYTHONPATH=/workspaces/powerchoice src/.venv/bin/python -c \"\nfrom src.utils.ai.prompts import pairwise_messages, _DEFAULT_SINGLE, _DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION, _DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING\n# check defaults contain choice_1/2 and not choice_line\nprint('DEFAULT_SINGLE contains choice_1:', '{choice_1}' in _DEFAULT_SINGLE, '{choice_2}' in _DEFAULT_SINGLE, '{choice_line}' in _DEFAULT_SINGLE)\nprint('DEFAULT_SINGLE:', repr(_DEFAULT_SINGLE[-80:]))\n# single with allow True\nmsgs = pairwise_messages(problem_title='P', problem_description='D', left_title='A', left_description='Desc A', right_title='B', right_description='Desc B', factor_title=None, factor_description=None, group_type='alternative', allow_skip_tie=True)\ncontent = msgs[1]['content']\nprint('--- single allow True content tail ---')\nprint(content[-300:])\nassert '1. ' in content and '2. ' in content, 'missing prefixes'\nassert '1. Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP' in content\nassert '2. Respond with 1, 2, TIE or SKIP' in content\n# check successive lines: '1. ...\\n2. '\nassert '1. Respond' in content\n# find lines\nlines = content.split('\\n')\n# last two lines should be 1. and 2.\nprint('last2:', lines[-2], '|', lines[-1])\nassert lines[-2].startswith('1. ')\nassert lines[-1].startswith('2. ')\nprint('single True OK')\n\nmsgs2 = pairwise_messages(problem_title='P', problem_description='D', left_title='A', left_description='Desc A', right_title='B', right_description='Desc B', factor_title=None, factor_description=None, group_type='alternative', allow_skip_tie=False)\nc2 = msgs2[1]['content']\nprint('--- single allow False tail ---')\nprint(c2[-300:])\nassert '1. Respond with Option 1 or Option 2.' in c2\nassert '2. summary — Prompting for AI agent voting\n\n**Commit:** `43eda5a` — `feat(ai): migrate AI voting prompts to choice_1/choice_2 with numbered prefixes`\n\n**Major touch points:**\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:13` — `VERSION` bump `0.7.77` → `0.7.78`\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:37-55` — placeholder comments updated from `{choice_line}` to `{choice_1} {choice_2}`; three template defaults (`AI_PROMPT_SINGLE_OPTION_TEMPLATE`, `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_OPTION_TEMPLATE`, `AI_PROMPT_FACTOR_RANKING_TEMPLATE`) changed from `…\\n\\n{choice_line}` to `…\\n\\n1. {choice_1}\\n2. {choice_2}`\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:82-108` — `_DEFAULT_SINGLE`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_OPTION`, `_DEFAULT_FACTOR_RANKING` mirrored to `1. {choice_1}\\n2. {choice_2}`\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:110-145` — added `_DEPRECATED_CHOICE_LINE_WARNED`, `_sanitize_choice_line()` (strips newlines/whitespace to preserve `1.`/`2.` layout) and `_maybe_warn_legacy_choice_line()` (logs once via `utils_show` when `{choice_line}` detected)\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:148-185` — `pairwise_messages()` docstring updated to list `choice_1, choice_2` (legacy noted), computes `selected = tie_skip/strict`, `sanitized = _sanitize_choice_line(selected)`, `choice_1 = choice_2 = choice_line = sanitized` for dual support\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:200-215`, `223-245`, `260-275` — all three branches (criteria factor-ranking, factor-option, single) now build `params` with `choice_1`, `choice_2`, `choice_line`, call `_maybe_warn_legacy_choice_line(tpl)` before `_safe_format`/`tpl.format`, ensuring `1. {choice_1}\\n2. {choice_2}` rendered on successive lines (single `\\n`, no blank) and legacy `{choice_line}` auto-fallback\n- `docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD:75-96` — ambient param tables updated to `{choice_1} {choice_2}`, added deprecation note for `{choice_line}`\n- `docs_examples/AI_PARTICIPANT_README.MD:98-160` — default prompt blocks changed to `1. {choice_1}\\n2. {choice_2}` and choice-line description expanded to show per-choice duplicated rendering and migration note\n\n**Verification:** `python -c` checks confirmed defaults contain `choice_1`/`choice_2` not `choice_line`, `pairwise_messages` for single/factor/criteria with `allow_skip_tie=True/False` emits final two lines `1. …` then `2. …` with correct quota-switched text, legacy `choice_line` template still renders via fallback with deprecation log; `pytest src/tests/test_ai_agents.py` — 27
